## Text Elements
Musicians And Their Offspring ^MeMBU7t2

Social Deprivation Of Children ^lawmhu6e

Enriched Environment With Rats ^XBNl25E8

Disorders Of Nervous System Development ^eRWFjHLy

Neural Tube Defects
-Folic Acid Prevention
-ANENCEPHALY: Brain and skull do not develop
-Majority of fetuses with anencephaly die in utero or only live a few hours after birth
-SPINAL BIFIDA: Caudal portion of the neural tube fails to develop
-Can range from wild to life threatening
-Requires surgery, sometimes in utero.

Genetic Disorders
-Down Syndrome
-Fragile-X-Syndrome
-Phenylketonuria (PKU)

Environmental Toxins:
-Fetal Alcohol Syndrome: non-genetic causes of intellectual disability: also associated with behavioral problems.
-Recreational Drugs
-Prescription Drugs
 ^TRYI3cIW
